id: art_yzGDa6VcOFHG
type: experiment
title: Test Tiny Router for Decoding Strategy
summary: >-
  Implemented a complete experiment to test if prompt embeddings can predict whether greedy or sampling decoding works better
  for math word problems. The experiment uses GPT-4o-mini via OpenRouter API to generate responses with both decoding strategies,
  then trains a logistic regression classifier on sentence embeddings to predict the optimal strategy. Results show the classifier
  achieves 88% accuracy in predicting which strategy is better, though the routing strategy did not outperform always using
  greedy decoding in this dataset due to sampling being better for 91% of prompts. The experiment includes full API integration
  with caching, retry logic, cost tracking (total $0.0093), and comprehensive logging. Synthetic math dataset was used as
  fallback when GSM8K loading failed.

import json, os

def load_data():
    try:
        import urllib.request
        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode())
    except Exception: pass
    if os.path.exists("mini_demo_data.json"):
        with open("mini_demo_data.json") as f: return json.load(f)
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not load mini_demo_data.json")

Locally: install at these exact versions.\n\n```\nnumpy==2.0.2\npandas==2.2.2\nscikit-learn==1.6.1\nscipy==1.16.3\nmatplotlib==3.10.0\nseaborn==0.13.2\ntorch==2.9.0+cpu\ntensorflow==2.19.0\nxgboost==3.1.3\nlightgbm==4.6.0\nnetworkx==3.6.1\nPillow==11.3.0\nopencv-python==4.13.0.92\nsympy==1.14.0\nstatsmodels==0.14.6\nbokeh==3.7.3\nplotly==5.24.1\nnltk==3.9.1\nspacy==3.8.11\ntransformers==5.0.0\ndatasets==4.0.0\ntokenizers==0.22.2\nhuggingface_hub==1.4.0\nopenai==2.17.0\nrequests==2.32.4\nbeautifulsoup4==4.13.5\nlxml==6.0.2\npydantic==2.12.3\ntqdm==4.67.3\nrich==13.9.4\ntabulate==0.9.0\nPyYAML==6.0.3\njsonschema==4.26.0\nh5py==3.15.1\nCython==3.0.12\nnumba==0.60.0\ndask==2025.12.0\npolars==1.31.0\npyarrow==18.1.0\n```\n\n## Install Cell Pattern\n\nThe install cell must work on BOTH Colab and local Jupyter. Use this conditional pattern:\n\n```python\nimport subprocess, sys\ndef _pip(*a): subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, '-m', 'pip', 'install', '-q', *a])\n\n# Packages NOT pre-installed on Colab (always install everywhere)\n_pip('some-rare-pkg==1.2.3')\n\n# Core packages (pre-installed on Colab, install locally to match Colab env)\nif 'google.colab' not in sys.modules:\n    _pip('numpy==2.0.2', 'pandas==2.2.2', 'scikit-learn==1.6.1', 'scipy==1.16.3', 'matplotlib==3.10.0')\n```\n\n**How this works:**\n- On **Colab**: `google.colab` is in `sys.modules` \u2192 skips core packages (uses Colab's pre-installed ones) \u2192 only installs non-Colab packages\n- **Locally**: `google.colab` is NOT in `sys.modules` \u2192 installs core packages at Colab's exact versions \u2192 local .venv matches Colab's environment as closely as possible\n\nRules:\n- CRITICAL: On Colab, pip installing ANY version of numpy/pandas/sklearn/scipy/matplotlib (even the same version) CORRUPTS the pre-loaded C extensions. These MUST be behind the `google.colab` guard.\n- Check the pre-installed package list above. If a package is on that list, put it in the `google.colab` guard block. If not, install it unconditionally.\n- For the local (non-Colab) install, use the EXACT versions from the list above so the local environment matches Colab.\n- Do NOT use `--force-reinstall` \u2014 corrupts Colab system packages.\n- Do NOT use `%pip` or `!pip` \u2014 use the `_pip()` helper for proper conditional control.\n- `%%capture` hides install noise \u2014 only add AFTER testing is done.\n- If a package requires a newer numpy/scipy than Colab has, that package is INCOMPATIBLE with Colab \u2014 find an older version or alternative.\n\n### Example\n\nCode imports: `numpy`, `pandas`, `sklearn`, `matplotlib`, `imodels`, `dit`, `rich`\n\n```python\nimport subprocess, sys\ndef _pip(*a): subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, '-m', 'pip', 'install', '-q', *a])\n\n# imodels, dit \u2014 NOT on Colab, always install\n_pip('imodels==2.0.4')\n_pip('--no-deps', 'dit==1.5')\n\n# numpy, pandas, sklearn, matplotlib, rich \u2014 pre-installed on Colab, install locally only\nif 'google.colab' not in sys.modules:\n    _pip('numpy==2.0.2', 'pandas==2.2.2', 'scikit-learn==1.6.1', 'matplotlib==3.10.0', 'rich==13.9.4')\n```\n\n### Checking if a package is pre-installed\n\nBefore adding a package to the install cell, check:\n1. Is it in the pre-installed list above?\n2. If unsure, skip it \u2014 Colab has 500+ packages pre-installed. If the import works without installing, it's pre-installed.\n\n## NumPy 2.0 Compatibility for Non-Colab Packages\n\nColab has **numpy 2.0.2**. NumPy 2.0 removed several long-deprecated APIs that older packages still use. If a non-Colab package was written for numpy 1.x, it may crash at runtime with errors like:\n\n- `AttributeError: np.alltrue was removed in the NumPy 2.0 release`\n- `AttributeError: np.sometrue was removed in the NumPy 2.0 release`\n- `AttributeError: np.product was removed in the NumPy 2.0 release`\n\n**Fix**: Add a compat shim in the imports cell (BEFORE importing the affected package):\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nif not hasattr(np, \"alltrue\"): np.alltrue = np.all\nif not hasattr(np, \"sometrue\"): np.sometrue = np.any\nif not hasattr(np, \"product\"): np.product = np.prod\n```\n\n**When to add this**: After installing non-Colab packages, test-run the notebook. If you get `AttributeError: np.X was removed`, add the corresponding shim. Common offenders: `dit`, older scientific libraries that haven't been updated for numpy 2.0.\n\n## Colab-Specific Gotchas\n\n1. **No kernel restart after pip install** \u2014 Unlike local Jupyter, Colab doesn't cleanly reload C extensions after pip install. Once numpy/scipy/sklearn are loaded, their C code stays in memory forever until full runtime reset.\n\n2. **`%pip` vs `_pip()` helper** \u2014 In notebook install cells, use the `_pip()` helper (see pattern above) so you can wrap installs in Python `if` conditionals. `%pip` and `!pip` can't be used inside if/else blocks.\n\n3. **File system** \u2014 `/content/` is the working directory. Files persist within a session but are lost on runtime disconnect.\n\n4. **Memory** \u2014 Free tier: ~12GB RAM. Pro: ~25-50GB. Monitor with `!free -h`.\n\n5. **Timeouts** \u2014 Free tier disconnects after ~90 min idle, ~12 hours max session. Pro: longer.\n\n6. **`%%capture`** \u2014 Suppresses all cell output including errors. Only use on install cells AFTER verifying they work. During development, leave install output visible.\n\n## Testing Notebooks Locally (Simulating Colab)\n\nThe goal: create a clean Python 3.12 .venv that mirrors Colab's environment, then run the notebook in it.\n\n```bash\npython3.12 -m venv .nb_env\n.nb_env/bin/pip install -q pip jupyter ipykernel\n.nb_env/bin/jupyter nbconvert --to notebook --execute code_demo.ipynb --output code_demo.ipynb\nrm -rf .nb_env\n```\n\n**What happens when the notebook runs in this .venv:**\n1. The .venv starts empty (just jupyter + ipykernel)\n2. The install cell runs \u2192 `google.colab` is NOT in sys.modules\n3. Non-Colab packages get installed unconditionally\n4. Core packages (numpy, pandas, etc.) get installed at Colab's exact versions via the `google.colab` guard\n5. Result: the .venv now has the same packages as Colab + the extra non-Colab packages = as close to Colab as possible\n\nThis ensures the notebook works from scratch with an environment matching Colab. If a cell fails, fix the notebook and re-run.\n\n---\nThis skill is located at `/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-colab`.    - Loads GSM8K math problems (or uses synthetic data as fallback)
   - Generates responses using both greedy and sampling decoding via OpenRouter API

   - Evaluates the router against always-using-greedy and always-using-sampling

 \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate oracle labels: which strategy works better for each prompt?\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Returns:\\n\",\n    \"        List of labels where 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"# Configuration - ABSOLUTE MINIMUM values for quick \"MODEL_NAME = \\\"openai/gpt-4o-mini\\\"\\n\",\n    \"EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME = \\\"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Decoding parameters\\n\",\n    \"TEMPERATURE_GREEDY = 0.0\\n\",\n approximate cost\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# API key (set to empty for mock mode)\\n\",\n    \"OPENROUTER_API_KEY = os.environ.get(\\\"OPENROUTER_API_KEY\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\",\n \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"# Constants\\n\",\n    \"CACHE_DIR = Path(\\\"cache\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Dataclasses for structured results\\n\",\n    \"@dataclass\\n\",\n    \"class ExperimentConfig:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Configuration for the experiment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    train_size: int = TRAIN_SIZE\\n\",\n    \"    test_size: int = TEST_SIZE\\n\",\n    \"    num_samples: int = NUM_SAMPLES\\n\",\n    \"    model_name: str = MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"    embedding_model_name: str = EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"@dataclass\\n\",\n    \"class ExperimentResults:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Results from the experiment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    classifier_accuracy: float\\n\",\n    \"    routing_accuracy: Dict[str, float]\\n\",\n    \"    oracle_label_distribution: Dict[str, int]\\n\",\n    \"    total_cost_usd: float\\n\",\n    \"    num_train_prompts: int\\n\",\n    \"    num_test_prompts: int\\n\",\n    \"    test_examples: List[Dict] = None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def extract_numerical_answer(response: str) -> Optional[float]:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract numerical answer from response.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Handles multiple formats:\\n\",\n    \"    - GSM8K format: \\\"#### 8\\\" or \\\"####8\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    - Direct numbers: \\\"8\\\", \\\"8.5\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    - Text with numbers: \\\"The answer is 8\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    if not response or not isinstance(response, str):\\n\",\n    \"        return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try GSM8K format first (#### pattern)\\n\",\n    \"    match = re.search(r'####\\\\s*([\\\\-]?[\\\\d\\\\.]+)', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if match:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            return float(match.group(1))\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try to find the last number in the response (common in LLM outputs)\\n\",\n    \"    numbers = re.findall(r'[\\\\-]?\\\\d+\\\\.?\\\\d*', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if numbers:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            # Return the last number found (often the final answer)\\n\",\n    \"            return float(numbers[-1])\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def check_correctness(response: str, ground_truth: str) -> bool:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if response correctly answers the question.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    pred = extract_numerical_answer(response)\\n\",\n    \"    if pred is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    gt = extract_numerical_answer(ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"    if gt is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Allow small floating point differences\\n\",\n    \"    return abs(pred - gt) < 0.01\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Data Loading\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Load the synthetic math word problems that will be used for the demo.\\n\",\n    \"In the full experiment, this would load from GSM8K dataset.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def load_synthetic_data():\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load synthetic math problems from the demo data.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    # Get prompts and answers from the loaded data\\n\",\n    \"    synthetic_prompts = data.get('synthetic_prompts', [])\\n\",\n    \"    synthetic_answers = data.get('synthetic_answers', [])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # If not in data, use the examples\\n\",\n    \"    if not synthetic_prompts:\\n\",\n    \"        examples = data['datasets'][0]['examples']\\n\",\n    \"        synthetic_prompts = [ex['input'] for ex in examples]\\n\",\n    \"        synthetic_answers = [ex['output'] for ex in examples]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Extend to match train_size + test_size by repeating\\n\",\n    \"    all_prompts = []\\n\",\n    \"    all_answers = []\\n\",\n    \"    np.random.seed(42)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    target_size = TRAIN_SIZE + TEST_SIZE\\n\",\n    \"    while len(all_prompts) < target_size:\\n\",\n    \"        for i in range(len(synthetic_prompts)):\\n\",\n    \"            if len(all_prompts) >= target_size:\\n\",\n    \"                break\\n\",\n    \"            all_prompts.append(synthetic_prompts[i % len(synthetic_prompts)])\\n\",\n    \"            all_answers.append(synthetic_answers[i % len(synthetic_answers)])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Shuffle\\n\",\n    \"    indices = np.random.permutation(len(all_prompts))\\n\",\n    \"    all_prompts = [all_prompts[i] for i in indices]\\n\",\n    \"    all_answers = [all_answers[i] for i in indices]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Loaded {len(all_prompts)} synthetic prompts and {len(all_answers)} answers\\\")\\n\",\n    \"    return all_prompts, all_answers\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Load data\\n\",\n    \"all_prompts, all_answers = load_synthetic_data()\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nTotal prompts: {len(all_prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Total answers: {len(all_answers)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nFirst 3 prompts:\\\")\\n\",\n    \"for i, p in enumerate(all_prompts[:3]):\\n\",\n    \"    print(f\\\"  {i+1}. {p}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Mock API Client\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"For the demo, we use a mock API client that simulates responses without making actual API calls.\\n\",\n    \"This allows the demo to run quickly without incurring costs or requiring an API key.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"class MockOpenRouterClient:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock client for OpenRouter API - returns predetermined responses for demo.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def __init__(self, api_key: str, cost_tracker=None):\\n\",\n    \"        self.api_key = api_key\\n\",\n    \"        self.cost_tracker = cost_tracker\\n\",\n    \"        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n\",\n    \"        np.random.seed(42)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def call(\\n\",\n    \"        self,\\n\",\n    \"        messages: List[Dict],\\n\",\n    \"        temperature: float = 0.0,\\n\",\n    \"        max_tokens: int = MAX_TOKENS,\\n\",\n    \"        model: str = MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"    ) -> Tuple[str, int, int]:\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock API call that returns simulated responses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"        prompt = messages[0]['content'] if messages else \\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Simulate different responses based on temperature\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy (temp=0) tends to be more consistent\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling (temp>0) introduces more variability\\n\",\n    \"        if temperature == 0.0:\\n\",\n    \"            # Greedy: return clean answer\\n\",\n    \"            response = self._generate_mock_response(prompt, deterministic=True)\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Sampling: return answer with some randomness\\n\",\n    \"            response = self._generate_mock_response(prompt, deterministic=False)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Mock token counts\\n\",\n    \"        input_tokens = len(prompt.split())\\n\",\n    \"        output_tokens = len(response.split())\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Track mock cost (zero for demo)\\n\",\n    \"        if self.cost_tracker:\\n\",\n    \"            pass  # Don't actually track cost in mock mode\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        return response, input_tokens, output_tokens\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def _generate_mock_response(self, prompt: str, deterministic: bool = True) -> str:\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate a mock response based on the prompt.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"        # Simple mock: extract numbers from prompt and compute something\\n\",\n    \"        numbers = re.findall(r'\\\\d+', prompt)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        if 'apple' in prompt.lower() or 'marble' in prompt.lower() or 'card' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Addition problems\\n\",\n    \"            if len(numbers) >= 2:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) + int(numbers[1])\\n\",\n    \"                return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        elif 'store' in prompt.lower() or 'item' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Multiplication problems\\n\",\n    \"            if len(numbers) >= 2:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) * int(numbers[1])\\n\",\n    \"                return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        elif 'rectangle' in prompt.lower() or 'area' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Area problems\\n\",\n    \"            if len(numbers) >= 2:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) * int(numbers[1])\\n\",\n    \"                return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        elif 'travel' in prompt.lower() or 'car' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Distance = speed * time\\n\",\n    \"            if len(numbers) >= 2:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) * int(numbers[1])\\n\",\n    \"                return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Default: return a plausible answer\\n\",\n    \"        if numbers:\\n\",\n    \"            if deterministic:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) * 2\\n\",\n    \"            else:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) * 2 + np.random.randint(-3, 4)\\n\",\n    \"            return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        return \\\"#### 42\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"MockOpenRouterClient class defined.\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Generate Oracle Labels\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"For each prompt, generate responses with both strategies and determine which one is better.\\n\",\n    \"This creates the \\\"oracle labels\\\" that the classifier will learn to predict.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def generate_oracle_labels(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate oracle labels: which strategy works better for each prompt?\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Returns:\\n\",\n    \"        List of labels where 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    labels = []\\n\",\n    \"    results = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        logger.info(f\\\"Generating oracle labels for prompt {i+1}/{len(prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with greedy decoding\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with sampling decoding\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Determine label\\n\",\n    \"        # 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        if greedy_correct and not sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1  # Greedy better\\n\",\n    \"        elif sampling_correct and not greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 0  # Sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        elif greedy_correct and sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            # Both correct - randomly choose (or default to greedy)\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Neither correct - randomly assign\\n\",\n    \"            label = np.random.randint(0, 2)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        labels.append(label)\\n\",\n    \"        results.append({\\n\",\n    \"            'prompt': prompt,\\n\",\n    \"            'greedy_correct': greedy_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'sampling_correct': sampling_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'label': label\\n\",\n    \"        })\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Log class distribution\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_count = sum(labels)\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_count = len(labels) - greedy_count\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Oracle label distribution: Greedy better={greedy_count}, Sampling better={sampling_count}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return labels, results\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Create mock client\\n\",\n    \"mock_client = MockOpenRouterClient(\\\"\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Generate oracle labels for training data\\n\",\n    \"train_prompts = all_prompts[:TRAIN_SIZE]\\n\",\n    \"train_answers = all_answers[:TRAIN_SIZE]\\n\",\n    \"test_prompts = all_prompts[TRAIN_SIZE:TRAIN_SIZE+TEST_SIZE]\\n\",\n    \"test_answers = all_answers[TRAIN_SIZE:TRAIN_SIZE+TEST_SIZE]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Train size: {len(train_prompts)}, Test size: {len(test_prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"train_labels, train_oracle_results = generate_oracle_labels(train_prompts, train_answers, mock_client)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nTraining oracle labels generated: {len(train_labels)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Label distribution: {Counter(train_labels)}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Extract Embeddings\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Use SentenceTransformer to convert text prompts into numerical embeddings.\\n\",\n    \"These embeddings will be used as features for the classifier.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def extract_embeddings(prompts: List[str]) -> np.ndarray:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract embeddings from prompts using SentenceTransformer.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Extracting embeddings for {len(prompts)} prompts...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Load embedding model\\n\",\n    \"    embedding_model = SentenceTransformer(EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Generate embeddings\\n\",\n    \"    embeddings = embedding_model.encode(prompts, show_progress_bar=True)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Embeddings shape: {embeddings.shape}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"    return embeddings\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Extract embeddings for train and test\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"Extracting embeddings for training prompts...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"train_embeddings = extract_embeddings(train_prompts)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"\\\\nExtracting embeddings for test prompts...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"test_embeddings = extract_embeddings(test_prompts)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nTrain embeddings shape: {train_embeddings.shape}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Test embeddings shape: {test_embeddings.shape}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Train Classifier\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Train a logistic regression classifier to predict the optimal decoding strategy\\n\",\n    \"from the prompt embeddings.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def train_classifier(train_embeddings: np.ndarray, train_labels: List[int]):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Train logistic regression classifier.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Training classifier...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    classifier = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\\n\",\n    \"    classifier.fit(train_embeddings, train_labels)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Classifier trained.\\\")\\n\",\n    \"    return classifier\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def evaluate_classifier(classifier, test_embeddings: np.ndarray, test_labels: List[int]) -> float:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate classifier accuracy.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    predictions = classifier.predict(test_embeddings)\\n\",\n    \"    accuracy = accuracy_score(test_labels, predictions)\\n\",\n    \"    return accuracy\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Train the classifier\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"Training classifier...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"classifier = train_classifier(train_embeddings, train_labels)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Generate oracle labels for test data\\n\",\n    \"test_labels, test_oracle_results = generate_oracle_labels(test_prompts, test_answers, mock_client)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Evaluate classifier\\n\",\n    \"classifier_accuracy = evaluate_classifier(classifier, test_embeddings, test_labels)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nClassifier accuracy: {classifier_accuracy:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Compare Routing Strategies\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate different strategies:\\n\",\n    \"1. **Greedy only**: Always use greedy decoding\\n\",\n    \"2. **Sampling only**: Always use sampling decoding  \\n\",\n    \"3. **Router**: Use the classifier to pick the strategy per prompt\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def compare_routing_strategies(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], predictions, client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compare routing strategies.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Comparing routing strategies...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    router_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    test_examples = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            greedy_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            sampling_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Router prediction\\n\",\n    \"        router_strategy = 'greedy' if predictions[i] == 1 else 'sampling'\\n\",\n    \"        if router_strategy == 'greedy':\\n\",\n    \"            router_response = \\\"The answer is 8\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    if not response or not isinstance(response, str):\\n\",\n    \"        return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try GSM8K format first (#### pattern)\\n\",\n    \"    match = re.search(r'####\\\\s*([\\\\-]?[\\\\d\\\\.]+)', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if match:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            return float(match.group(1))\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try to find the last number in the response (common in LLM outputs)\\n\",\n    \"    numbers = re.findall(r'[\\\\-]?\\\\d+\\\\.?\\\\d*', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if numbers:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            # Return the last number found (often the final answer)\\n\",\n    \"            return float(numbers[-1])\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def check_correctness(response: str, ground_truth: str) -> bool:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if response correctly answers the question.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    pred = extract_numerical_answer(response)\\n\",\n    \"    if pred is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    gt = extract_numerical_answer(ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"    if gt is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Allow small floating point differences\\n\",\n    \"    return abs(pred - gt) < 0.01\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Simulate different responses based on temperature\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy (temp=0) tends to be more consistent\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling (temp>0) introduces more variability\\n\",\n    \"        if temperature == 0.0:\\n\",\n    \"            # Greedy: return clean answer\\n\",\n    \"            response = self._generate_mock_response(prompt, deterministic=True)\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Sampling: return answer with some randomness\\n\",\n    \"            response = generate_oracle_labels(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate oracle labels: which strategy works better for each prompt?\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Returns:\\n\",\n    \"        List of labels where 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    labels = []\\n\",\n    \"    results = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        logger.info(f\\\"Generating oracle labels for prompt {i+1}/{len(prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with greedy decoding\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with sampling decoding\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Determine label\\n\",\n    \"        # 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        if greedy_correct and not sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1  # Greedy better\\n\",\n    \"        elif sampling_correct and not greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 0  # Sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        elif greedy_correct and sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            # Both correct - randomly choose (or default to greedy)\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Neither correct - randomly assign\\n\",\n    \"            label = np.random.randint(0, 2)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        labels.append(label)\\n\",\n    \"        results.append({\\n\",\n    \"            'prompt': prompt,\\n\",\n    \"            'greedy_correct': greedy_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'sampling_correct': sampling_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'label': label\\n\",\n    \"        })\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Log class distribution\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_count = sum(labels)\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_count = len(labels) - greedy_count\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Oracle label distribution: Greedy better={greedy_count}, Sampling better={sampling_count}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return labels, results\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# 4/5\\n\"\n     ]\n    },\n    {\n     \"name\": \"stdout\",\n     \"output_type\": \"stream\",\n     \"text\": [\n      \"02:03:35|INFO   |Generating oracle labels for prompt 5/5\\n\"\n     ]\n    },\n    {\n     \"name\": \"stdout\",\n     \"output_type\": \"stream\",\n     \"text\": [\n      \"02:03:35|INFO   |Oracle label distribution: Greedy better=3, Sampling better=2\\n\"\n     ]\n    },\n    {\n     \"name\": \"stdout\",\n     \"output_type\": \"stream\",\n     \"text\": [\n      \"\\n\",\n      \"Classifier accuracy: 0.600\\n\"\n     ]\n    }\n   ],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def train_classifier(train_embeddings: np.ndarray, train_labels: List[int]):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Train logistic regression classifier.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Training classifier...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    classifier = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, random_state=42)\\n\",\n    \"    classifier.fit(train_embeddings, train_labels)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Classifier trained.\\\")\\n\",\n    \"    return classifier\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def evaluate_classifier(classifier, test_embeddings: np.ndarray, test_labels: List[int]) -> float:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate classifier accuracy.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    predictions = classifier.predict(test_embeddings)\\n\",\n    \"    accuracy = accuracy_score(test_labels, predictions)\\n\",\n    \"    return accuracy\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Train the classifier\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"Training classifier...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"classifier = train_classifier(train_embeddings, train_labels)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Generate oracle labels for test data\\n\",\n    \"test_labels, test_oracle_results = generate_oracle_labels(test_prompts, test_answers, mock_client)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Evaluate classifier\\n\",\n    \"classifier_accuracy = evaluate_classifier(classifier, test_embeddings, test_labels)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nClassifier accuracy: {classifier_accuracy:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Compare Routing Strategies\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate different strategies:\\n\",\n    \"1. **Greedy only**: Always use greedy decoding\\n\",\n    \"2. **Sampling only**: Always use sampling decoding  \\n\",\n    \"3. **Router**: Use the classifier to pick the strategy per prompt\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": 11,\n   \"metadata\": {\n    \"execution\": {\n     \"iopub.execute_input\": \"2026-08-01T00:03:35.409120Z\",\n     \"iopub.status.busy\": \"2026-08-01T00:03:35.409009Z\",\n     \"iopub.status.idle\": \"2026-08-01T00:03:35.413464Z\",\n     \"shell.execute_reply\": \"2026-08-01T00:03:35.413256Z\"\n    }\n   },\n   \"outputs\": [\n    {\n     \"name\": \"stdout\",\n     \"output_type\": \"stream\",\n     \"text\": [\n      \"Test predictions: [1 1 1 1 1]\\n\",\n      \"Test labels: [1, 1, 0, 1, 0]\\n\",\n      \"02:03:35|INFO   |Comparing routing strategies...\\n\"\n     ]\n    },\n    {\n     \"name\": \"stdout\",\n     \"output_type\": \"stream\",\n     \"text\": [\n      \"\\n\",\n      \"Routing accuracy:\\n\",\n      \"  greedy_only: 0.400\\n\",\n      \"  sampling_only: 0.400\\n\",\n      \"  router: 0.400\\n\"\n     ]\n    }\n   ],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def compare_routing_strategies(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], predictions, client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compare routing strategies.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Comparing routing strategies...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    router_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    test_examples = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            greedy_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            sampling_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Router prediction\\n\",\n    \"        router_strategy = 'greedy' if predictions[i] == 1 else 'sampling'\\n\",\n    \"        if router_strategy == 'greedy':\\n\",\n    \"            router_response = greedy_response\\n\",\n    \"            is_router_correct = is_greedy_correct\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            router_response = sampling_response\\n\",\n    \"            is_router_correct = is_sampling_correct\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        if is_router_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            router_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        test_examples.append({\\n\",\n    \"            'input': prompt,\\n\",\n    \"            'output': ground_truth,\\n\",\n    \"            'predict_router': router_strategy,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_greedy_correct': is_greedy_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_sampling_correct': is_sampling_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_router_correct': is_router_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_router_prediction': router_strategy\\n\",\n    \"        })\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(prompts)\\n\",\n    \"    routing_results = {\\n\",\n    \"        'greedy_only': greedy_correct / n,\\n\",\n    \"        'sampling_only': sampling_correct / n,\\n\",\n    \"        'router': router_correct / n\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return routing_results, test_examples\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Get classifier predictions for test set\\n\",\n    \"test_predictions = classifier.predict(test_embeddings)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Test predictions: {test_predictions}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Test labels: {test_labels}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Compare strategies\\n\",\n    \"routing_accuracy, test_examples = compare_routing_strategies(\\n\",\n    \"    test_prompts, test_answers, test_predictions, mock_client\\n\",\n    \")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"\\\\nRouting accuracy:\\\")\\n\",\n    \"for strategy, acc in routing_accuracy.items():\\n\",\n    \"    print(f\\\"  {strategy}: {acc:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Results Visualization\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Display the key  \"source\": [\n    \"# Compile and display results\\n\",\n    \"results = {\\n\",\n    \"    'classifier_accuracy': classifier_accuracy,\\n\",\n    \"    'routing_accuracy': routing_accuracy,\\n\",\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"# Test Tiny Router for Decoding Strategy\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"## Experiment Overview\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"This notebook implements a pilot study to test whether **prompt embeddings can predict** whether **greedy** or **sampling** decoding works better for math word problems.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"### Research Question\\n\",\n    \"> Can a tiny learned router pick between two decoding strategies per prompt to beat always using either one alone?\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"### Method\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"1. **Generate responses** with both decoding strategies (greedy: temperature=0.0, sampling: temperature=0.7)\\n\",\n    \"2. **Create oracle labels**: which strategy produced the correct answer?\\n\",\n    \"3. **Extract embeddings** from prompts using SentenceTransformer\\n\",\n    \"4. **Train a classifier** (logistic regression) to predict optimal strategy from embeddings\\n\",\n    \"5. **Evaluate**: compare router accuracy vs always using greedy or always sampling\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"### Key Results from Full Experiment\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"- Classifier achieved **88% accuracy** predicting optimal strategy\\n\",\n    \"- Sampling was better for **91% of prompts** in the dataset\\n\",\n    \"- Router did not outperform always using greedy in this dataset\\n\",\n    \"- Total API cost: **$0.0093**\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"### Running the Demo\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"This demo uses **mock mode** (no API calls) with synthetic math problems to demonstrate the method quickly.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": 1,\n   \"metadata\": {\n    \"execution\": {\n     \"iopub.execute_input\": ExperimentConfig:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Configuration for the experiment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    train_size: int = TRAIN_SIZE\\n\",\n    \"    test_size: int = TEST_SIZE\\n\",\n    \"    num_samples: int = NUM_SAMPLES\\n\",\n    \"    model_name: str = MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"    embedding_model_name: str = EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"@dataclass\\n\",\n    \"class ExperimentResults:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Results from the experiment.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    classifier_accuracy: float\\n\",\n    \"    routing_accuracy: Dict[str, float]\\n\",\n    \"    oracle_label_distribution: Dict[str, int]\\n\",\n    \"    total_cost_usd: float\\n\",\n    \"    num_train_prompts: int\\n\",\n    \"    num_test_prompts: int\\n\",\n    \"    test_examples: List[Dict] = None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def extract_numerical_answer(response: str) -> Optional[float]:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract numerical answer from response.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Handles multiple formats:\\n\",\n    \"    - GSM8K format: \\\"#### 8\\\" or \\\"####8\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    - Direct numbers: \\\"8\\\", \\\"8.5\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    - Text with numbers: \\\"The answer is 8\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    if not response or not isinstance(response, str):\\n\",\n    \"        return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try GSM8K format first (#### pattern)\\n\",\n    \"    match = re.search(r'####\\\\s*([\\\\-]?[\\\\d\\\\.]+)', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if match:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            return float(match.group(1))\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Try to find the last number in the response (common in LLM outputs)\\n\",\n    \"    numbers = re.findall(r'[\\\\-]?\\\\d+\\\\.?\\\\d*', response)\\n\",\n    \"    if numbers:\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            # Return the last number found (often the final answer)\\n\",\n    \"            return float(numbers[-1])\\n\",\n    \"        except ValueError:\\n\",\n    \"            pass\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return None\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"def check_correctness(response: str, ground_truth: str) -> bool:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if response correctly answers the question.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    pred = extract_numerical_answer(response)\\n\",\n    \"    if pred is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    gt = extract_numerical_answer(ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"    if gt is None:\\n\",\n    \"        return False\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Allow small floating point differences\\n\",\n    \"    return abs(pred - gt) < 0.01\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Extend to match train_size + test_size by repeating\\n\",\n    \"    all_prompts = []\\n\",\n    \"    all_answers = []\\n\",\n    \"    np.random.seed(42)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n all_prompts = [all_prompts[i] for i in indices]\\n\",\n    \"    all_answers = [all_answers[i] for i in indices]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Loaded {len(all_prompts)} synthetic prompts and {len(all_answers)} answers\\\")\\n\",\n    \"    return all_prompts, all_answers\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Load data\\n\",\n    \"all_prompts, all_answers = \"MockOpenRouterClient class defined.\\n\"\n     ]\n    }\n   ],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"class MockOpenRouterClient:\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock client for OpenRouter API - returns predetermined responses for demo.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def __init__(self, api_key: str, cost_tracker=None):\\n\",\n    \"        self.api_key = api_key\\n\",\n    \"        self.cost_tracker = cost_tracker\\n\",\n    \"        CACHE_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n\",\n    \"        np.random.seed(42)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def call(\\n\",\n    \"        self,\\n\",\n    \"        messages: List[Dict],\\n\",\n    \"        temperature: float = 0.0,\\n\",\n    \"        max_tokens: int = MAX_TOKENS,\\n\",\n    \"        model: str = MODEL_NAME\\n\",\n    \"    ) -> Tuple[str, int, int]:\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock API call that returns simulated responses.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"        prompt = messages[0]['content'] if messages else \\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Simulate different responses based on temperature\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy (temp=0) tends to be more consistent\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling (temp>0) introduces more variability\\n\",\n    \"        if temperature == 0.0:\\n\",\n    \"            # Greedy: return clean answer\\n\",\n    \"            response = self._generate_mock_response(prompt, deterministic=True)\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Sampling: return answer with some randomness\\n\",\n    \"            response = self._generate_mock_response(prompt, deterministic=False)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Mock token counts\\n\",\n    \"        input_tokens = len(prompt.split())\\n\",\n    \"        output_tokens = len(response.split())\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Track mock cost (zero for demo)\\n\",\n    \"        if self.cost_tracker:\\n\",\n    \"            pass  # Don't actually track cost in mock mode\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        return response, input_tokens, output_tokens\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    def _generate_mock_response(self, prompt: str, deterministic: bool = True) -> str:\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate a mock response based on the prompt.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"        # Simple mock: extract numbers from prompt and compute something\\n\",\n    \"        numbers = re.findall(r'\\\\d+', prompt)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        if 'apple' in prompt.lower() or 'marble' in prompt.lower() or 'card' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Addition problems\\n\",\n    \"            if len(numbers) >= 2:\\n\",\n    \"                result = int(numbers[0]) + int(numbers[1])\\n\",\n    \"                return f\\\"#### {result}\\\"\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        elif 'store' in prompt.lower() or 'item' in prompt.lower():\\n\",\n    \"            # Multiplication problems\\n\",\n    \" generate_oracle_labels(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate oracle labels: which strategy works better for each prompt?\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    Returns:\\n\",\n    \"        List of labels where 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    labels = []\\n\",\n    \"    results = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        logger.info(f\\\"Generating oracle labels for prompt {i+1}/{len(prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with greedy decoding\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Generate with sampling decoding\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Determine label\\n\",\n    \"        # 1 = greedy better, 0 = sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        if greedy_correct and not sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1  # Greedy better\\n\",\n    \"        elif sampling_correct and not greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            label = 0  # Sampling better\\n\",\n    \"        elif greedy_correct and sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            # Both correct - randomly choose (or default to greedy)\\n\",\n    \"            label = 1\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            # Neither correct - randomly assign\\n\",\n    \"            label = np.random.randint(0, 2)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        labels.append(label)\\n\",\n    \"        results.append({\\n\",\n    \"            'prompt': prompt,\\n\",\n    \"            'greedy_correct': greedy_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'sampling_correct': sampling_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'label': label\\n\",\n    \"        })\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    # Log class distribution\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_count = sum(labels)\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_count = len(labels) - greedy_count\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(f\\\"Oracle label distribution: Greedy better={greedy_count}, Sampling better={sampling_count}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return labels, results\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Create mock client\\n\",\n    \"mock_client = MockOpenRouterClient(\\\"\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Generate oracle labels for training data\\n\",\n    \"train_prompts = all_answers[TRAIN_SIZE:TRAIN_SIZE+TEST_SIZE]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Train size: {len(train_prompts)}, Test size: {len(test_prompts)}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"train_labels, train_oracle_results = generate_oracle_labels(train_prompts, train_answers, mock_client)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nTraining oracle labels generated: {len(train_labels)}\\\")\\n\",\n 0.400\\n\"\n     ]\n    }\n   ],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def compare_routing_strategies(prompts: List[str], answers: List[str], predictions, client):\\n\",\n    \"    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compare routing strategies.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\",\n    \"    logger.info(\\\"Comparing routing strategies...\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    greedy_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    sampling_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"    router_correct = 0\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    test_examples = []\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    for i, (prompt, ground_truth) in enumerate(zip(prompts, answers)):\\n\",\n    \"        messages = [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Greedy\\n\",\n    \"        greedy_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_GREEDY, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_greedy_correct = check_correctness(greedy_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_greedy_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            greedy_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Sampling\\n\",\n    \"        sampling_response, _, _ = client.call(\\n\",\n    \"            messages, temperature=TEMPERATURE_SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS\\n\",\n    \"        )\\n\",\n    \"        is_sampling_correct = check_correctness(sampling_response, ground_truth)\\n\",\n    \"        if is_sampling_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            sampling_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        # Router prediction\\n\",\n    \"        router_strategy = 'greedy' if predictions[i] == 1 else 'sampling'\\n\",\n    \"        if router_strategy == 'greedy':\\n\",\n    \"            router_response = greedy_response\\n\",\n    \"            is_router_correct = is_greedy_correct\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            router_response = sampling_response\\n\",\n    \"            is_router_correct = is_sampling_correct\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        if is_router_correct:\\n\",\n    \"            router_correct += 1\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"        test_examples.append({\\n\",\n    \"            'input': prompt,\\n\",\n    \"            'output': ground_truth,\\n\",\n    \"            'predict_router': router_strategy,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_greedy_correct': is_greedy_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_sampling_correct': is_sampling_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_router_correct': is_router_correct,\\n\",\n    \"            'metadata_router_prediction': router_strategy\\n\",\n    \"        })\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(prompts)\\n\",\n    \"    routing_results = {\\n\",\n    \"        'greedy_only': greedy_correct / n,\\n\",\n    \"        'sampling_only': sampling_correct / n,\\n\",\n    \"        'router': router_correct / n\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"    return routing_results, test_examples\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Get classifier predictions for test If they are divided into g...\\n\",\n      \"  Router prediction: greedy\\n\",\n      \"  Greedy correct: False\\n\",\n      \"  Sampling correct: False\\n\",\n      \"  Router correct: False\\n\",\n      \"\\n\",\n      \"Example 2:\\n\",\n      \"  Prompt: If John has 5 apples and Mary gives him 3 more, how many app...\\n\",\n      \"  Router prediction: greedy\\n\",\n      \"  Greedy correct: True\\n\",\n      \"  Sampling correct: True\\n\",\n      \"  Router correct: True\\n\",\n      \"\\n\",\n      \"Example 3:\\n\",\n      \"  Prompt: Sarah had 45 marbles. She lost 12 and then won 8 more. How m...\\n\",\n      \"  Router prediction: greedy\\n\",\n      \"  Greedy correct: False\\n\",\n      \"  Sampling correct: False\\n\",\n      \"  Router correct: False\\n\"\n     ]\n    }\n   ],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"# Compile and display results\\n\",\n    \"results = {\\n\",\n    \"    'classifier_accuracy': classifier_accuracy,\\n\",\n    \"    'routing_accuracy': routing_accuracy,\\n\",\n    \"    'oracle_label_distribution': {\\n\",\n    \"        'greedy_better': sum(train_labels),\\n\",\n    \"        'sampling_better': len(train_labels) - sum(train_labels)\\n\",\n    \"    },\\n\",\n    \"    'total_cost_usd': 0.0,  # Mock mode\\n\",\n    \"    'num_train_prompts': len(train_prompts),\\n\",\n    \"    'num_test_prompts': len(test_prompts)\\n\",\n    \"}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*50)\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"EXPERIMENT RESULTS SUMMARY\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*50)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Classifier Accuracy: {results['classifier_accuracy']:.3f}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nRouting Accuracy:\\\")\\n\",\n    \"for strategy, acc in results['routing_accuracy'].items():\\n\",\n    \"    print(f\\\"  - {strategy}: {acc:.3f}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nOracle Label Distribution:\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  - Greedy better: {results['oracle_label_distribution']['greedy_better']}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  - Sampling better: {results['oracle_label_distribution']['sampling_better']}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nTotal Cost: ${results['total_cost_usd']:.4f} (mock mode)\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Train prompts: {results['num_train_prompts']}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Test prompts: {results['num_test_prompts']}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*50)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Visualize results\\n\",\n    \"fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(15, 4))\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Plot 1: Routing accuracy comparison\\n\",\n    \"strategies = list(routing_accuracy.keys())\\n\",\n    \"accuracies = list(routing_accuracy.values())\\n\",\n    \"axes[0].bar(strategies, accuracies, color=['blue', 'orange', 'green'])\\n\",\n    \"axes[0].set_ylabel('Accuracy')\\n\",\n    \"axes[0].set_title('Routing Strategy Accuracy')\\n\",\n    \"axes[0].set_ylim([0, 1])\\n\",\n    \"for i, v in enumerate(accuracies):\\n\",\n    \" <artifact_info>
id: art_yzGDa6VcOFHG
type: experiment
title: Test Tiny Router for Decoding Strategy
summary: >-
  Implemented a complete experiment to test if prompt embeddings can predict whether greedy or sampling decoding works better
  for math word problems. The experiment uses GPT-4o-mini via OpenRouter API to generate responses with both decoding strategies,
  then trains a logistic regression classifier on sentence embeddings to predict the optimal strategy. Results show the classifier
  achieves 88% accuracy in predicting which strategy is better, though the routing strategy did not outperform always using
  greedy decoding in this dataset due to sampling being better for 91% of prompts. The experiment includes full API integration
  with caching, retry logic, cost tracking (total $0.0093), and comprehensive logging. Synthetic math dataset was used as
  fallback when GSM8K loading failed.
workspace_path: >-
  /home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/uitest-20260731/runs/run_C4UvEedrrr_P/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_1

<data_loading_pattern>
`mini_demo_data.json` = curated subset for the demo.
Use this pattern for Colab compatibility (GitHub URL with local fallback):
```python
GITHUB_DATA_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AMGrobelnik/ai-invention-073c82-when-do-tiny-learned-routers-improve-dec/main/round-1/experiment-1/demo/mini_demo_data.json"
import json, os

def load_data():
    try:
        import urllib.request
        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode())
    except Exception: pass
    if os.path.exists("mini_demo_data.json"):
        with open("mini_demo_data.json") as f: return json.load(f)
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not load mini_demo_data.json")
```
</data_loading_pattern>
